## Methodology

1. Extracted `title` from YAML frontmatter (falls back to first heading, then filename)
2. Computed pairwise title similarity using `difflib.SequenceMatcher`
3. Flagged pairs exceeding the similarity threshold
4. For flagged pairs, computed similarity on first 200 characters of body content
5. Results sorted by title similarity (highest first)
